This bug causes the threshold.foreground setting to be \" \\\n        f\"ignored by Chromium.\"\n\n    # Make sure the old key is NOT present\n    assert 'TextBrightnessThreshold' not in setting_keys, \\\n        f\"Old key 'TextBrightnessThreshold' should not be used in Qt 6.4. \" \\\n        f\"This would cause the threshold setting to be silently ignored. \" \\\n        f\"Got keys: {setting_keys}\"\n\n    # Qt 6.4 versions\n    qt64_versions = version.WebEngineVersions.from_pyqt('6.4.0')\n    qt64_settings = darkmode.settings(versions=qt64_versions, special_flags=[])\n    qt64_keys = [key for key, _ in qt64_settings.get('dark-mode-settings', [])\n                 + qt64_settings.get('blink-settings', [])]\n\n    # Qt 6.3 should use TextBrightnessThreshold\n    assert 'TextBrightnessThreshold' in qt63_keys, \\\n        f\"Qt 6.3 should use 'TextBrightnessThreshold'. Got: {qt63_keys}\"\n\n    # Qt 6.4 should use ForegroundBrightnessThreshold (the renamed key)\n    assert 'ForegroundBrightnessThreshold' in qt64_keys, \\\n        f\"Qt 6.4 should use 'ForegroundBrightnessThreshold'. {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-haiku-4-5-20251001","id":"msg_01XcVwrUqZ3txxRs7g2RpMp2","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_011vARiMzQk8R8WXgqfTN4jK","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/workspace/TEST_DOCUMENTATION.md","content":"# Qt 6.4 Dark Mode Text Threshold Setting Bug - Regression Test\n\n## Problem Summary\n\nWhen users configure the dark mode text brightness threshold setting (`colors.webpage.darkmode.threshold.foreground`) in qutebrowser running with Qt 6.4, the setting has **no effect** on dark mode rendering, even though the same setting works correctly on Qt 6.3 and earlier.\n\n## Root Cause\n\nIn Qt 6.4, the Chromium dark mode implementation **renamed** one of its internal settings:\n- **Old name (Qt 5.15 - 6.3):** `TextBrightnessThreshold`\n- **New name (Qt 6.4+):** `ForegroundBrightnessThreshold`\n\nIf qutebrowser continues to use the old key name `TextBrightnessThreshold` when generating dark mode settings for Qt 6.4, Chromium will **silently ignore** the setting because it no longer recognizes that key name. This makes the threshold setting appear to have no effect.\n\n## Code Location\n\nThe bug is in: `qutebrowser/browser/webengine/darkmode.py`\n\nSpecifically, in the `_DEFINITIONS` dictionary and how the Qt 6.4 variant is constructed:\n\n```python\n# Line 333-335 in darkmode.py\n_DEFINITIONS[Variant.qt_64] = _DEFINITIONS[Variant.qt_515_3].copy_replace_setting(\n    'threshold.foreground', 'ForegroundBrightnessThreshold',\n)\n```\n\nThis code should correctly replace the old key with the new one. However, if the bug exists, it means:\n1. Either `copy_replace_setting()` is not working correctly, or\n2. The Qt 6.4 definition is incorrectly still using `TextBrightnessThreshold`\n\n## Regression Test Details\n\n### File: `/workspace/regression_test.py`\n\nThe test file contains three assertions that verify the fix:\n\n### Test 1: `test_qt64_threshold_foreground_uses_correct_chromium_key()`\n\n**Purpose:** Verify that Qt 6.4 uses the correct new Chromium key name.\n\n**Assertion:**\n- Gets the Qt 6.4 darkmode definition\n- Finds the `threshold.foreground` setting\n- Asserts that `chromium_key == 'ForegroundBrightnessThreshold'`\n\n**Expected Outcome:**\n- ✓ PASS if the bug is fixed (Qt 6.4 uses the new key)\n- ✗ FAIL if the bug exists (Qt 6.4 still uses the old `TextBrightnessThreshold` key)\n\n### Test 2: `test_qt64_does_not_use_old_text_threshold_key()`\n\n**Purpose:** Explicitly verify that the old key is NOT present in Qt 6.4 definition.\n\n**Assertion:**\n- Collects all chromium keys in the Qt 6.4 definition\n- Asserts that `'TextBrightnessThreshold'` is NOT in the list\n\n**Expected Outcome:**\n- ✓ PASS if the bug is fixed (old key removed)\n- ✗ FAIL if the bug exists (old key still present)\n\n### Test 3: `test_qt64_threshold_differs_from_qt63()`\n\n**Purpose:** Verify that the key name is different between Qt 6.3 and Qt 6.4 (as it should be).\n\n**Assertions:**\n- Qt 6.3 uses `TextBrightnessThreshold`\n- Qt 6.4 uses `ForegroundBrightnessThreshold`\n- The two are different\n\n**Expected Outcome:**\n- ✓ PASS if the bug is fixed (keys are different as expected)\n- ✗ FAIL if the bug exists (both use the same old key)\n\n## How to Run the Test\n\n### From the qutebrowser repository root:\n\n```bash\npython3 -m pytest /workspace/regression_test.py -v\n```\n\n### Expected Results\n\nIf the bug is **NOT fixed** (current buggy code):\n```\ntest_qt64_threshold_foreground_uses_correct_chromium_key FAILED\ntest_qt64_does_not_use_old_text_threshold_key FAILED\ntest_qt64_threshold_differs_from_qt63 FAILED\n```\n\nIf the bug **IS fixed**:\n```\ntest_qt64_threshold_foreground_uses_correct_chromium_key PASSED\ntest_qt64_does_not_use_old_text_threshold_key PASSED\ntest_qt64_threshold_differs_from_qt63 PASSED\n```\n\n## Technical Details\n\nThe test accesses the internal `darkmode._DEFINITIONS` dictionary and examines the `_Setting` objects:\n\n```python\n# Each variant (qt_515_3, qt_64, etc.) has a _Definition with _Settings\n_DEFINITIONS[Variant.qt_64]._settings\n```\n\nFor each setting, the test checks:\n- `setting.option`: The qutebrowser configuration option name (e.g., 'threshold.foreground')\n- `setting.chromium_key`: The key name passed to Chromium (e.g., 'ForegroundBrightnessThreshold')\n\n## Understanding the Impact\n\n### User Perspective\n1. This makes the threshold setting appear to have no effect.\n\n## Code Location\n\nThe bug is in: `qutebrowser/browser/webengine/darkmode.py`\n\nSpecifically, in the `_DEFINITIONS` dictionary and how the Qt 6.4 variant is constructed:\n\n```python\n# Line 333-335 in darkmode.py\n_DEFINITIONS[Variant.qt_64] = _DEFINITIONS[Variant.qt_515_3].copy_replace_setting(\n    'threshold.foreground', 'ForegroundBrightnessThreshold',\n)\n```\n\nThis code should correctly replace the old key with the new one. However, if the bug exists, it means:\n1. Either `copy_replace_setting()` is not working correctly, or\n2. The Qt 6.4 definition is incorrectly still using `TextBrightnessThreshold`\n\n## Regression Test Details\n\n### File: `/workspace/regression_test.py`\n\nThe test file contains three assertions that verify the fix:\n\n### Test 1: `test_qt64_threshold_foreground_uses_correct_chromium_key()`\n\n**Purpose:** Verify that Qt 6.4 uses the correct new Chromium key name.\n\n**Assertion:**\n- Gets the Qt 6.4 darkmode definition\n- Finds the `threshold.foreground` setting\n- Asserts that `chromium_key == 'ForegroundBrightnessThreshold'`\n\n**Expected Outcome:**\n- ✓ PASS if the bug is fixed (Qt 6.4 uses the new key)\n- ✗ FAIL if the bug exists (Qt 6.4 still uses the old `TextBrightnessThreshold` key)\n\n### Test 2: `test_qt64_does_not_use_old_text_threshold_key()`\n\n**Purpose:** Explicitly verify that the old key is NOT present in Qt 6.4 definition.\n\n**Assertion:**\n- Collects all chromium keys in the Qt 6.4 definition\n- Asserts that `'TextBrightnessThreshold'` is NOT in the list\n\n**Expected Outcome:**\n- ✓ PASS if the bug is fixed (old key removed)\n- ✗ FAIL if the bug exists (old key still present)\n\n### Test 3: `test_qt64_threshold_differs_from_qt63()`\n\n**Purpose:** Verify that the key name is different between Qt 6.3 and Qt 6.4 (as it should be).\n\n**Assertions:**\n- Qt 6.3 uses `TextBrightnessThreshold`\n- Qt 6.4 uses `ForegroundBrightnessThreshold`\n- The two are different\n\n**Expected Outcome:**\n- ✓ PASS if the bug is fixed (keys are different as expected)\n- ✗ FAIL if
